codex
Finding D3-R1-01: Unhashed graph fields can be modified and propagated while retaining the original graphId
  category: 1. Authoritative graph boundary
  evidence: `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:229-274` hashes only the D2 ID projection, while `:1044-1057` and `:1169-1175` consume unhashed `cannotConclude`, `limitations`, and `provenance`. Reproduction changed freshness, `cannotConclude`, and `provenance.sanitizedCount` on an authentic graph without changing graphId; D3 returned `valid:true`, propagated `["CANNOT_CONCLUDE"]`, and emitted sanitizedCount `777777`.
  proposed closure: Validate the complete D2 graph schema and invariants, and bind every consumed field into an authenticated snapshot identity. A publicly recomputable diagnostic hash alone cannot prove D2 origin.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-02: Duplicate node IDs can count as multiple independent evidence units
  category: 2. Correlation double-count
  evidence: `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:653-680` counts every unmatched occurrence as a solo unit, while `:368-385` never rejects duplicate node IDs or malformed correlation membership. A recomputed-hash graph containing node `a` twice and no correlation groups was accepted with supporting IDs `["a","a"]`, `soloSupportCount:2`, and confidence raised to `moderate/60`.
  proposed closure: Revalidate D2 uniqueness, semantic-deduplication, correlation partition, membership completeness, canonical group IDs, and independence weights before evaluation.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-03: Authentic D2 contradiction edges originating at matched evidence are ignored
  category: 3. Contradiction suppression
  evidence: `contracts/r3.0d/evidence-node-contract.js:67-68` defines `contradictingEdges` as other evidence contradicting the node; D2 materializes it as `from=node, to=other` at `renderer/js/r3-0d-evidence-graph.js:1183-1188`. D3 only counts edges whose `to` is matched at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:634-648`. Reproduction produced `{from:"a",to:"b",kind:"contradicts"}`, but hypothesis `a` had `contradictingEvidenceIds:[]`, status `supported`, score `30`.
  proposed closure: Normalize the edge semantics and count the endpoint representing “other evidence contradicting this matched node”; add a genuine D2→D3 contradiction test.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-04: Fabricated imported-summary evidence can be elevated to Measured
  category: 5. Credibility laundering / 12. Imported summary elevation
  evidence: D2 rejects this combination at `renderer/js/r3-0d-evidence-graph.js:1046-1049`, but D3 does not repeat that invariant before `_aggregateCredibility` at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:689-702`. A graph with `sourceId:"imported_summary"` and `credibility:"measured"` plus a correctly recomputed graphId was accepted and emitted `credibility:"Measured"`.
  proposed closure: Revalidate every node using the D1/D2 contracts and explicitly enforce the imported-summary elevation prohibition.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-05: D3 is not freshness-aware and accepts arbitrarily stale evidence unchanged
  category: 10. Stale graph
  evidence: `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:704-708` hard-codes `staleContribution = 0` and states there is no freshness check. Freshness is also excluded from `_projectIdPayload` at `:231-237`. Replacing freshness with `1900-01-01T00:00:00Z` retained graphId and returned `valid:true`. This contradicts `governance/r3.0d/checkpoints/D3.json:42`.
  proposed closure: Validate freshness shape and enforce the directive’s age policy using an injected reference clock; deterministically downweight or block stale evidence.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-06: Node lapId mismatches are accepted
  category: 11. Case/session mismatch
  evidence: `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:368-385` checks caseId and sessionId only. A recomputed-hash graph with case lap `L1` and node lap `L2` returned `valid:true`. D2’s required rule is at `renderer/js/r3-0d-evidence-graph.js:1043-1045`.
  proposed closure: Apply D2’s lap rule verbatim: when both association and node lap IDs are non-null, require equality.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-07: Clone-time accessors execute before the original graph is structurally audited
  category: 15. Self-restoring replacements / 1. Authoritative graph boundary
  evidence: D3 invokes `HI.safeStructuredClone(gIn)` at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:304-310`, then audits only the clone at `:321-325`. The HI contract explicitly warns at `contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js:382-385` that structuredClone invokes input traps and requires an original-shape audit first. A shallow-frozen graph can therefore contain nested getters that execute and disappear into clone data properties.
  proposed closure: Perform descriptor-only recursive shape/accessor/symbol validation on the original before cloning, then audit the clone again.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-08: Ambient Object.isFrozen is directly callable and bypasses the frozen-input requirement
  category: 14. Ambient intrinsic calls
  evidence: Direct ambient calls survive at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:66`, `:93`, and `:173`. Rebinding `Object.isFrozen = () => true` caused a completely mutable authentic graph clone to pass and return `valid:true`. Section J mutates `Object.freeze`, not `Object.isFrozen`.
  proposed closure: Add a captured `safeObjectIsFrozen` HI wrapper and use it everywhere, with regression and ambient-rebinding tests.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-09: Section J does not mutation-protect every HI wrapper called by D3
  category: 16. HI wrapper coverage / 17. Mutation-test effectiveness
  evidence: D3 calls wrappers including `safeArrayForEach`, `safeArraySlice`, `safeGetOwnDescriptor`, `safeHasOwn`, `safeDefineDataProperty`, `safeObjectCreateNull`, `safeRegExpTest`, `safeStructuredClone`, `safeUtf8ByteLength`, and `deepOriginalShapeAudit`; Section J at `tests/r3.0d-hypothesis-engine.test.js:525-633` covers only ten ambient bindings. For example, regressing `HI.safeArraySlice` at production lines `911-937` to `.slice()` is not exercised under a hostile `Array.prototype.slice`, so that mutant is not forced to EXIT 1.
  proposed closure: Add one structurally effective source-regression/ambient-rebinding test for every runtime HI wrapper and verify each mutant independently fails the suite.
  severity: BLOCK

Finding D3-R1-10: The post-clock integrity guard does not detect most ambient mutations
  category: 19. Hostile clock
  evidence: `_intrinsicsIntact` at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:86-95` checks only whether the HI API object appears frozen. Consequently, a clock rebinding `Array.prototype.push` is not detected at `:1163-1166`, although captured wrappers prevent that specific mutation from corrupting local state.
  proposed closure: Either extend the guard to verify captured ambient identities or narrow the documented claim to HI API integrity and rely explicitly on captured wrappers.
  severity: NIT

Finding D3-R1-11: ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP measures a reduced preview rather than the returned envelope
  category: 20. Oversized envelope
  evidence: The check at `renderer/js/r3-0d-hypothesis-engine.js:1142-1157` includes hypothesis IDs and correctly rejects only values strictly greater than the cap, but omits final `caseAssociation`, `sessionAssociation`, `provenance`, `createdAt`, `generationToken`, and `contextVersion` added at `:1178-1198`.
  proposed closure: Measure the exact final envelope immediately before freezing/returning, excluding only fields explicitly exempted by contract.
  severity: NIT

The remaining categories—4, 6, 7, 8, 9, 13, and 18—showed no independent defect: scores remain bounded integers; hidden top-level confidence keys are rejected; emitted i18n strings are registry-controlled; output arrays are sorted; the registry/API are frozen; raw observation params/source freshness are not emitted; and ordinary exceptions collapse to the structured blocked result. Category 19’s clock mutation does not currently corrupt local state, but its guard claim is overstated as noted above.

FINAL VERDICT: BLOCK
